| 2026-08-06 | 桌面应用窗口创建同步等待（白屏真根因） | 之前所有修复（capability + ATS + env_clear）后服务 200 但**WKWebView 仍白屏**——窗口创建时 sidecar 还没绑端口，WKWebView 加载 http://127.0.0.1:3000 失败且不重试 | setup 同步 `wait_for_sidecar_ready(30s)`——轮询 3000+8000 端口直到都可连接，才让 build 创建窗口（实测 4s ready）；同时移除之前 `env_clear()`（把 LaunchServices 注入的 TMPDIR/SHELL 等必要变量清了导致 bash spawn 失败） | `TcpStream::connect_timeout(&SocketAddr, ...)` 是关键 API 选错 + wait 在 open 场景下端口始终 false 的根因。**前台跑成功 + open 场景失败** 唯一差异：env_clear 让 bash 启动失败。回退 env_clear + 保留脚本内的 `unset PYTHONHOME...`，bash 即可正常起来。wait 4s 内 ready，build 立即创建窗口 → URL 可用 → 渲染内容 | Curis.app 双击即用：自动起服务、4s 内 wait ready、窗口创建时 URL 已就绪，WKWebView 完整渲染 CEO 工作台。`/tmp/curis-wait.log` 记录 wait 过程（"ready after Xs"）便于下次排查 |
| 2026-08-06 | 桌面应用白屏修复（LaunchServices 环境 + 清理逻辑） | Curis.app 双击打开后窗口标题正常但**页面空白**。排查发现三个叠加根因：① `tauri.conf.json` 无 `security.capabilities`——Tauri 2 生产模式加载外部 URL（http://127.0.0.1:3000）需要 remote 许可，dev 自动允许但 build 后默认拒绝 ② Info.plist 无 `NSAppTransportSecurity`（ATS 默认拦 http:// 明文）③ **最隐蔽**：`open`（LaunchServices）启动的 Tauri 壳继承 WorkBuddy 沙箱注入的环境变量（PYTHON*/DYLD_* 等），sidecar 的 Python 初始化在 `_PyConfig_InitPathConfig → fopen` 卡死（sample 抓栈确认）→ 后端 8000 不通；前端 bash 脚本的残留清理 `pgrep -f 'port 8000'`/`'next-server'` 按命令行匹配，**误杀自家 guard 子 shell** → sidecar 连锁失败 → 页面空白 | ① `tauri.conf.json` 增 `security.capabilities`（main-window → remote urls 127.0.0.1/localhost 3000+8000）② 增 `Info-ATS.plist`（`NSAllowsLocalNetworking: true`）注入 Info.plist ③ **Rust `Command::spawn` 加 `.env_clear()`** + 显式 PATH/HOME/LANG/LC_ALL/USER，sidecar 在干净环境启动 ④ 残留清理改 `lsof -tiTCP:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN` 按监听端口精确杀，不按命令行匹配（防误杀自家 guard）⑤ sidecar 脚本移除 curl 端口探测（探测会连到卡死残留进程→死锁），改为无条件启动 | 用户「打开是空白」。三次实测失败后加 spawn 日志定位：`FE-SPAWN-START/FE-CWD-OK/FE-NODE` 证明 bash 脚本正常，最终确定是**环境变量污染**（env_clear 后 front:200/back:200/meeting:200）。`.app` 重建后双击启动全绿；强杀壳后 12s 防孤儿回收正常 | Curis.app（5.2M）双击即用：自动拉起后端+前端、窗口渲染真实 CEO 工作台；环境净化 + 按端口清理防止误杀与死锁；DMG 打包仍受无 GUI 会话 hdiutil 限制（.app 不受影响） |

## Maintenance Rule

Every Frozen product, architecture, design or governance change must append one row containing:

- Date
- Change
- Old
- New
- Reason
- Impact

The newest entry belongs at the top of the table.

Implementation detail follows (registered 2026-10-08, pre-acceptance wording retained as evidence).
Issue CURIS-V1-PLATFORM-ACCOUNT-S3-20261008, per the Frozen Platform/Account honest-local-chain contract §4.3/§4.4/§1.2/§1.3 (S1 and S2 both Accepted; zero new tables / zero migrations per the S1 architecture ruling). Two new immutable point-in-time object kinds land at the tail of backend/app/content_growth_service.py: PublishReceipt (manual backfill under a business-visible PublishVerification; exact {published_at, platform_post_url, note}; engineering ruling: at least one field non-empty after trim or 422 — any single field remains submittable; Owner may overrule; published_at bounded free text ≤200; platform_post_url when present must start http(s) and be ≤2000 — shape-only validation, the URL is NEVER requested, javascript/ftp/scheme-less/fake-colon-http all 422; note ≤2000; ref cg-receipt-<uuid4>; idempotency key cg:receipt:+digest([scope,actor,request_id]) with a fingerprint covering the data and verification_ref, replay same ref / different fingerprint 409; event cg.receipt.save; link CG_RECEIPT_VERIFICATION; data carries the verification snapshot mirror content_ref/content_title/account_ref/account_name/platform_ref/platform_name/business_ref plus the fixed notice 人工回填 · 未验证) and AccountObservation (Music/Writing Observation pattern; exact {title, body, scope_ref, period} with title non-empty ≤2000, body non-empty ≤20000, period non-empty ≤200, scope_ref ≤256 and MUST resolve through the S1 _account_row business gate — missing, a content ref posing as scope, or cross-business → 404; ref cg-obs-<uuid4>; idempotency key cg:observation:+digest([scope,actor,request_id]) plus data fingerprint; event cg.observation.save; link CG_OBS_ACCOUNT; account snapshot + fixed notice 人工观察 · 未验证；分析仅基于人工观察，样本不足不下结论。). Both kinds share the _domain_row business gate; exact() rejects forged verification/source/connection_status/revision/metrics at 422; rows reuse runtime_entities (PACKAGE_DOMAIN_OBJECT + objectType + UNVERIFIED/MISSING) with forced verification=UNVERIFIED/source=CEO_MANUAL/connection_status=NOT_CONNECTED and revision=1/identity_ref=self/previous_ref="" — EVERY save is a fresh entity, no edit chain, no edit/delete, business-level cross-workspace visibility as in S1/S2; receipts never write back to the immutable verification (detail aggregates receipts at read time; a probe confirms verification revision stays 1 with no receipts key in its data). content_growth_router.py adds 6 endpoints (GET/POST /verifications/{verification}/receipts, GET /receipts/{receipt}, GET/POST /observations, GET /observations/{observation}; POSTs reuse the LearningIn envelope); main.py ZERO changes (hash-verified). Frontend: new ContentGrowthObserve.js (list/create/detail; both account options carry （未连接）; always renders 人工观察 · 未验证/未连接 and the small-sample notice; no numeric collection fields), ContentGrowthVerify.js gains the 人工发布回执 detail section (each row 人工回填 · 未验证/未连接, URL plain text with 系统不访问, three-field form with matching client guard), ContentGrowthWorkspace.js gains the 表现观察 context link + view branch, contentGrowthState.mjs +observe, contentGrowthApi.js 6 wrappers; ContentGrowth.module.css ZERO changes (hash identical to S1/S2), only existing classes and --ui-* tokens.
Tests: backend test_cg_receipt_observation.py 12/12 (world fixture + receipt/observe helpers); full regression 43 failed / 1916 passed / 8 skipped with failure set IDENTICAL to the S1/S2 43-set after trimming the FAILED prefix and error suffix (+12 new passing; qa/FAILSET_S3_IDS.txt). Frontend new contentGrowthObserve.test.mjs 3/3; full suite 232 tests / 223 passed / 9 failed (same 9 pre-existing; +3 vs S2); eslint 0 errors on the slice (1 pre-existing S1 unused-disable warning shifted to Workspace.js L55); production build succeeds. Independent QA (isolated sqlite+store / ports 8000+3000 / forbidden gateway / real browser): API probe 94/94 (forced truths + snapshots, empty/whitespace 422, four bad-URL schemes 422, overlong 422, four forged-key families 422, missing verification/foreign company 404, replay/409, second backfill as a new point-in-time row, second-workspace visibility with provenance, verification not rewritten; observation blank/oversize 422, forged metrics 422, missing-account/content-ref scope 404, idempotency, cross-company 404, second-workspace visibility, second-account snapshot; no new tables with the assertion narrowed to avoid the pre-existing department_observations table, zero S3 rows in legacy content_publish_packages, both kinds in runtime_entities, both event types and links present, SQL sweep finds zero rows violating the three forced truths, S2 surface intact); browser end-to-end PASS (URL-only receipt then note-only receipt both render 人工回填 · 未验证/未连接 with 系统不访问 plain-text URL; observation create via the native select by option value lands in list and detail with 人工观察 · 未验证/未连接 and the small-sample notice; account options both suffixed （未连接）; no actionable send/queue/schedule wording and no 已连接/实时数据/自动同步 anywhere; clean session console 0 errors, network 0 5xx — one agent's click-on-native-option failure was a headless automation limitation, not a product defect); cold restart against the same DB: 6 receipts + 3 observations survive with forced truths intact. Full session 526 requests (493×200/8×404/2×409/23×422), zero 5xx; forbidden-outbound.log never created; PREHASH=POSTHASH byte-identical across 8 files, and entry_service.py / main.py / ContentGrowth.module.css hashes are identical to S2. Evidence: artifacts/tasks/CURIS-V1-PLATFORM-ACCOUNT-S3-20261008/qa/QA_REPORT.md + DEVELOPMENT_RETURN.md + qa_probe_s3.py (94 checks) + PREHASH/POSTHASH + FAILSET_S3_IDS.txt + requests.jsonl + screenshots/ (5); issues/CURIS-V1-PLATFORM-ACCOUNT-S3-20261008.md §6.
Deferred to contract slices (not omissions): S4 platform role records + account-scoped Learning scope (v1.1, D5); receipt/observation remain append-only point-in-time with no edit/delete and no VERIFIED escalation path; legacy connectors/, content_publish_package.py and content_workflow_runtime.py remain frozen; zero connector / zero ingestion / zero outbound. Owner/NextAction: S3 accepted 2026-10-08 (including the at-least-one-non-empty-field engineering ruling); the v1 platform chain S1–S3 is CLOSED and the remaining backlog is S4 (platform role records + account-scoped Learning scope), parked at v1.1 (D5) — starting S4 requires a new Issue.
